#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct pair_hash {
    size_t operator()(const pair<int, int>& p) const {
        return p.first * 10007 + p.second;
    }
};

vector<pair<int, int>> directions = {{-1, 0}, {1, 0}, {0, -1}, {0, 1}};

vector<pair<int, int>> find_path(int n, const pair<int, int>& start, const pair<int, int>& end, const unordered_set<pair<int, int>, pair_hash>& forbidden) {
    vector<pair<int, int>> path;
    if (start == end) return path;

    queue<pair<int, int>> q;
    map<pair<int, int>, pair<int, int>> parent;

    q.push(start);
    parent[start] = {-1, -1};

    while (!q.empty()) {
        auto current = q.front();
        q.pop();

        for (const auto& dir : directions) {
            int nx = current.first + dir.first;
            int ny = current.second + dir.second;
            pair<int, int> next(nx, ny);

            if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > n)
                continue;
            if (forbidden.count(next))
                continue;
            if (parent.find(next) != parent.end())
                continue;

            parent[next] = current;
            q.push(next);

            if (next == end) {
                pair<int, int> curr = next;
                while (curr != start) {
                    path.push_back(curr);
                    curr = parent[curr];
                }
                reverse(path.begin(), path.end());
                return path;
            }
        }
    }
    return path;
}

vector<pair<int, int>> find_path_k(int n, const vector<vector<int>>& grid, const pair<int, int>& start, const pair<int, int>& end, const unordered_set<pair<int, int>, pair_hash>& forbidden) {
    vector<pair<int, int>> path;
    if (start == end) return path;

    queue<pair<int, int>> q;
    map<pair<int, int>, pair<int, int>> parent;

    q.push(start);
    parent[start] = {-1, -1};

    while (!q.empty()) {
        auto current = q.front();
        q.pop();

        for (const auto& dir : directions) {
            int nx = current.first + dir.first;
            int ny = current.second + dir.second;
            pair<int, int> next(nx, ny);

            if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > n)
                continue;
            if (forbidden.count(next))
                continue;
            if (grid[nx][ny] != -1)
                continue;
            if (parent.find(next) != parent.end())
                continue;

            parent[next] = current;
            q.push(next);

            if (next == end) {
                pair<int, int> curr = next;
                while (curr != start) {
                    path.push_back(curr);
                    curr = parent[curr];
                }
                reverse(path.begin(), path.end());
                return path;
            }
        }
    }
    return path;
}

pair<int, int> find_temporary_position(int n, const vector<vector<int>>& grid, const pair<int, int>& start, const unordered_set<pair<int, int>, pair_hash>& forbidden) {
    queue<pair<int, int>> q;
    map<pair<int, int>, bool> visited;

    q.push(start);
    visited[start] = true;

    while (!q.empty()) {
        auto current = q.front();
        q.pop();

        if (grid[current.first][current.second] == -1 && !forbidden.count(current)) {
            return current;
        }

        for (const auto& dir : directions) {
            int nx = current.first + dir.first;
            int ny = current.second + dir.second;
            pair<int, int> next(nx, ny);

            if (nx < 1 || nx > n || ny < 1 || ny > n)
                continue;
            if (forbidden.count(next))
                continue;
            if (visited.count(next))
                continue;

            visited[next] = true;
            q.push(next);
        }
    }

    for (int x = 1; x <= n; x++) {
        for (int y = 1; y <= n; y++) {
            pair<int, int> pos(x, y);
            if (grid[x][y] == -1 && !forbidden.count(pos)) {
                return pos;
            }
        }
    }
    return {-1, -1};
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;

    vector<pair<int, int>> initial(m), targets(m);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
        cin >> initial[i].first >> initial[i].second;
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
        cin >> targets[i].first >> targets[i].second;
    }

    vector<pair<int, int>> current = initial;
    vector<vector<int>> grid(n + 1, vector<int>(n + 1, -1));
    for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
        int x = current[i].first;
        int y = current[i].second;
        grid[x][y] = i;
    }

    vector<tuple<int, int, int, int>> moves;

    for (int i = m - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        auto& start = current[i];
        const auto& end = targets[i];
        if (start == end) continue;

        unordered_set<pair<int, int>, pair_hash> forbidden;
        for (int j = i + 1; j < m; j++) {
            forbidden.insert(targets[j]);
        }

        vector<pair<int, int>> path = find_path(n, start, end, forbidden);

        for (auto& step : path) {
            int nx = step.first, ny = step.second;
            if (grid[nx][ny] == -1) {
                moves.emplace_back(start.first, start.second, nx, ny);
                grid[start.first][start.second] = -1;
                grid[nx][ny] = i;
                start = {nx, ny};
            } else {
                int k = grid[nx][ny];
                unordered_set<pair<int, int>, pair_hash> forbidden_k;
                for (int j = i; j < m; j++) {
                    forbidden_k.insert(targets[j]);
                }
                pair<int, int> temp_pos = find_temporary_position(n, grid, current[k], forbidden_k);
                vector<pair<int, int>> k_path = find_path_k(n, grid, current[k], temp_pos, forbidden_k);

                for (auto& k_step : k_path) {
                    int kx1 = current[k].first, ky1 = current[k].second;
                    int kx2 = k_step.first, ky2 = k_step.second;
                    moves.emplace_back(kx1, ky1, kx2, ky2);
                    grid[kx1][ky1] = -1;
                    grid[kx2][ky2] = k;
                    current[k] = {kx2, ky2};
                }

                moves.emplace_back(start.first, start.second, nx, ny);
                grid[start.first][start.second] = -1;
                grid[nx][ny] = i;
                start = {nx, ny};
            }
        }
    }

    cout << moves.size() << '\n';
    for (const auto& move : moves) {
        int x1, y1, x2, y2;
        tie(x1, y1, x2, y2) = move;
        cout << x1 << ' ' << y1 << ' ' << x2 << ' ' << y2 << '\n';
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
int comb[51][51];

void precompute_comb() {
    for (int n = 0; n <= 50; ++n) {
        comb[n][0] = 1;
        comb[n][n] = 1;
        for (int k = 1; k < n; ++k) {
            comb[n][k] = (comb[n - 1][k - 1] + comb[n - 1][k]) % MOD;
        }
    }
}

int main() {
    precompute_comb();
    int n, k;
    cin >> n >> k;
    vector<int> weights(n);
    int a = 0, b = 0;
    for (int& w : weights) {
        cin >> w;
        if (w == 50) ++a;
        else ++b;
    }

    if ((a > 0 && 50 > k) || (b > 0 && 100 > k)) {
        cout << "-1\n0\n";
        return 0;
    }

    int distance[51][51][2];
    long long count[51][51][2];
    memset(distance, -1, sizeof(distance));
    memset(count, 0, sizeof(count));

    queue<tuple<int, int, int>> q;
    distance[a][b][0] = 0;
    count[a][b][0] = 1;
    q.emplace(a, b, 0);

    while (!q.empty()) {
        auto [x_curr, y_curr, pos] = q.front();
        q.pop();
        int curr_dist = distance[x_curr][y_curr][pos];
        long long curr_cnt = count[x_curr][y_curr][pos];

        if (pos == 0) {
            for (int x = 0; x <= x_curr; ++x) {
                for (int y = 0; y <= y_curr; ++y) {
                    if (x + y == 0 || 50 * x + 100 * y > k) continue;
                    int nx = x_curr - x, ny = y_curr - y;
                    if (nx < 0 || ny < 0) continue;
                    int new_pos = 1;
                    long long ways = (comb[x_curr][x] * comb[y_curr][y]) % MOD;
                    if (distance[nx][ny][new_pos] == -1) {
                        distance[nx][ny][new_pos] = curr_dist + 1;
                        count[nx][ny][new_pos] = (curr_cnt * ways) % MOD;
                        q.emplace(nx, ny, new_pos);
                    } else if (distance[nx][ny][new_pos] == curr_dist + 1) {
                        count[nx][ny][new_pos] = (count[nx][ny][new_pos] + curr_cnt * ways) % MOD;
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            int right_x = a - x_curr;
            int right_y = b - y_curr;
            for (int x = 0; x <= right_x; ++x) {
                for (int y = 0; y <= right_y; ++y) {
                    if (x + y == 0 || 50 * x + 100 * y > k) continue;
                    int nx = x_curr + x, ny = y_curr + y;
                    int new_pos = 0;
                    long long ways = (comb[right_x][x] * comb[right_y][y]) % MOD;
                    if (distance[nx][ny][new_pos] == -1) {
                        distance[nx][ny][new_pos] = curr_dist + 1;
                        count[nx][ny][new_pos] = (curr_cnt * ways) % MOD;
                        q.emplace(nx, ny, new_pos);
                    } else if (distance[nx][ny][new_pos] == curr_dist + 1) {
                        count[nx][ny][new_pos] = (count[nx][ny][new_pos] + curr_cnt * ways) % MOD;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    int res_dist = distance[0][0][1];
    long long res_cnt = res_dist == -1 ? 0 : count[0][0][1] % MOD;
    cout << res_dist << '\n' << res_cnt << '\n';

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Point {
    int x, y;
    int id;
    Point() {}
    Point(int x, int y, int id) : x(x), y(y), id(id) {}
};

int cross(const Point& a, const Point& b, const Point& c) {
    return (b.x - a.x) * (c.y - b.y) - (b.y - a.y) * (c.x - b.x);
}

bool intersect(const Point& a1, const Point& a2, const Point& b1, const Point& b2) {
    int c1 = cross(a1, a2, b1);
    int c2 = cross(a1, a2, b2);
    if ((c1 > 0 && c2 > 0) || (c1 < 0 && c2 < 0)) return false;
    int c3 = cross(b1, b2, a1);
    int c4 = cross(b1, b2, a2);
    if ((c3 > 0 && c4 > 0) || (c3 < 0 && c4 < 0)) return false;
    return true;
}

int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    vector<Point> points;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        int x, y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        points.emplace_back(x, y, i + 1);
    }
    string s;
    cin >> s;

    sort(points.begin(), points.end(), [](const Point& a, const Point& b) {
        return a.x < b.x || (a.x == b.x && a.y < b.y);
    });

    vector<int> perm;
    vector<bool> used(n, false);
    perm.push_back(0);
    perm.push_back(1);
    used[0] = used[1] = true;

    for (int i = 0; i < n - 2; ++i) {
        char turn = s[i];
        int a_idx = perm[perm.size() - 2];
        int b_idx = perm.back();
        const Point& a = points[a_idx];
        const Point& b = points[b_idx];

        int best_cr = 0;
        int best_candidate = -1;

        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            if (used[j]) continue;
            const Point& c = points[j];
            int cr = cross(a, b, c);
            if ((turn == 'L' && cr > 0) || (turn == 'R' && cr < 0)) {
                if (best_candidate == -1 || abs(cr) > abs(best_cr)) {
                    best_cr = cr;
                    best_candidate = j;
                }
            }
        }

        if (best_candidate == -1) {
            cout << -1 << endl;
            return 0;
        }

        perm.push_back(best_candidate);
        used[best_candidate] = true;
    }

    vector<Point> poly;
    for (int idx : perm) {
        poly.push_back(points[idx]);
    }

    bool ok = true;
    for (int i = 0; i < n - 1 && ok; ++i) {
        for (int j = i + 1; j < n - 1; ++j) {
            if (abs(i - j) == 1) continue;
            const Point& a = poly[i];
            const Point& b = poly[i + 1];
            const Point& c = poly[j];
            const Point& d = poly[j + 1];
            if (intersect(a, b, c, d)) {
                ok = false;
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    if (!ok) {
        cout << -1 << endl;
    } else {
        for (int idx : perm) {
            cout << points[idx].id << " ";
        }
        cout << endl;
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>

using namespace std;

vector<vector<int>> adj;
vector<int> degrees;

int find_furthest(int start) {
    int n = adj.size() - 1;
    vector<int> dist(n + 1, -1);
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(start);
    dist[start] = 0;
    int furthest = start, max_dist = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front();
        q.pop();
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (dist[v] == -1) {
                dist[v] = dist[u] + 1;
                if (dist[v] > max_dist) {
                    max_dist = dist[v];
                    furthest = v;
                }
                q.push(v);
            }
        }
    }
    return furthest;
}

vector<int> get_path(int start, int end) {
    int n = adj.size() - 1;
    vector<int> parent(n + 1, -1);
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(start);
    parent[start] = -1;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front();
        q.pop();
        if (u == end) break;
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (parent[v] == -1) {
                parent[v] = u;
                q.push(v);
            }
        }
    }
    vector<int> path;
    int current = end;
    while (current != start) {
        path.push_back(current);
        current = parent[current];
    }
    path.push_back(start);
    reverse(path.begin(), path.end());
    return path;
}

bool is_valid(int root) {
    int n = adj.size() - 1;
    vector<int> level(n + 1, -1);
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(root);
    level[root] = 0;
    bool valid = true;
    while (!q.empty() && valid) {
        int size = q.size();
        int expected_degree = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
            int u = q.front();
            q.pop();
            if (expected_degree == -1) {
                expected_degree = degrees[u];
            } else if (degrees[u] != expected_degree) {
                valid = false;
                break;
            }
            for (int v : adj[u]) {
                if (level[v] == -1) {
                    level[v] = level[u] + 1;
                    q.push(v);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return valid;
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n;
    cin >> n;

    if (n == 1) {
        cout << 1 << endl;
        return 0;
    }

    adj.resize(n + 1);
    degrees.resize(n + 1, 0);

    for (int i = 0; i < n - 1; ++i) {
        int u, v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        adj[u].push_back(v);
        adj[v].push_back(u);
        degrees[u]++;
        degrees[v]++;
    }

    int u = find_furthest(1);
    int v = find_furthest(u);
    vector<int> path = get_path(v, u);
    int len = path.size();

    vector<int> centers;
    if (len % 2 == 1) {
        centers.push_back(path[len / 2]);
    } else {
        centers.push_back(path[len / 2 - 1]);
        centers.push_back(path[len / 2]);
    }

    for (int c : centers) {
        if (is_valid(c)) {
            cout << c << endl;
            return 0;
        }
    }

    cout << -1 << endl;

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Pair {
    int group, prof, c;
    Pair(int g, int p, int cc) : group(g), prof(p), c(cc) {}
    bool operator<(const Pair& other) const { return c > other.c; }
};

int main() {
    int n, m, a;
    cin >> n >> m >> a;

    vector<vector<int>> c(n, vector<int>(m));
    vector<Pair> pairs;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
            cin >> c[i][j];
            if (c[i][j] > 0) {
                pairs.emplace_back(i, j, c[i][j]);
            }
        }
    }

    sort(pairs.begin(), pairs.end());

    vector<vector<vector<int>>> group_sched(n, vector<vector<int>>(6, vector<int>(7, 0)));
    vector<vector<vector<bool>>> prof_sched(m, vector<vector<bool>>(6, vector<bool>(7, false)));
    vector<vector<int>> classroom(6, vector<int>(7, 0));

    for (const auto& p : pairs) {
        int group = p.group;
        int prof = p.prof;
        int remaining = p.c;

        while (remaining > 0) {
            bool assigned = false;
            for (int d = 0; d < 6; ++d) {
                if (remaining == 0) break;
                int max_possible = min(remaining, 7);
                for (int k = max_possible; k >= 1; --k) {
                    for (int s = 0; s <= 7 - k; ++s) {
                        bool ok = true;
                        for (int slot = s; slot < s + k; ++slot) {
                            if (group_sched[group][d][slot] != 0 || prof_sched[prof][d][slot] || classroom[d][slot] >= a) {
                                ok = false;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                        if (ok) {
                            for (int slot = s; slot < s + k; ++slot) {
                                group_sched[group][d][slot] = prof + 1;
                                prof_sched[prof][d][slot] = true;
                                classroom[d][slot]++;
                            }
                            remaining -= k;
                            assigned = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    if (assigned) break;
                }
                if (assigned) break;
            }
        }
    }

    int total_fatigue = 0;

    for (int g = 0; g < n; ++g) {
        for (int d = 0; d < 6; ++d) {
            int first = -1, last = -1;
            for (int s = 0; s < 7; ++s) {
                if (group_sched[g][d][s]) {
                    if (first == -1) first = s;
                    last = s;
                }
            }
            if (first != -1) {
                int spread = last - first + 1;
                total_fatigue += (2 + spread) * (2 + spread);
            }
        }
    }

    for (int p = 0; p < m; ++p) {
        for (int d = 0; d < 6; ++d) {
            int first = -1, last = -1;
            for (int s = 0; s < 7; ++s) {
                if (prof_sched[p][d][s]) {
                    if (first == -1) first = s;
                    last = s;
                }
            }
            if (first != -1) {
                int spread = last - first + 1;
                total_fatigue += (2 + spread) * (2 + spread);
            }
        }
    }

    cout << total_fatigue << endl << endl;

    for (int g = 0; g < n; ++g) {
        for (int s = 0; s < 7; ++s) {
            for (int d = 0; d < 6; ++d) {
                cout << group_sched[g][d][s];
                if (d != 5) cout << ' ';
            }
            cout << endl;
        }
        if (g != n-1) cout << endl;
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Triangulation {
    int n;
    set<pair<int, int>> diagonals;

    Triangulation(int _n) : n(_n) {}

    void add_diagonal(int a, int b) {
        if (a > b) swap(a, b);
        diagonals.insert({a, b});
    }

    bool has_diagonal(int a, int b) {
        if (a > b) swap(a, b);
        if (abs(a - b) == 1 || (a == 1 && b == n)) return true; // polygon edge
        return diagonals.count({a, b});
    }

    void remove_diagonal(int a, int b) {
        if (a > b) swap(a, b);
        diagonals.erase({a, b});
    }

    pair<int, int> find_flip(int a, int b) {
        vector<int> neighbors_a, neighbors_b;
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
            if (i == a) continue;
            if (has_diagonal(a, i)) neighbors_a.push_back(i);
        }
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
            if (i == b) continue;
            if (has_diagonal(b, i)) neighbors_b.push_back(i);
        }

        sort(neighbors_a.begin(), neighbors_a.end(), [&](int x, int y) {
            int dx = (x - a + n) % n;
            int dy = (y - a + n) % n;
            return dx < dy;
        });
        sort(neighbors_b.begin(), neighbors_b.end(), [&](int x, int y) {
            int dx = (b - x + n) % n;
            int dy = (b - y + n) % n;
            return dx < dy;
        });

        int c = -1, d = -1;
        for (int x : neighbors_a) {
            if (has_diagonal(b, x)) {
                c = x;
                break;
            }
        }
        for (int x : neighbors_b) {
            if (has_diagonal(a, x)) {
                d = x;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (c == -1 || d == -1) {
            return {-1, -1};
        }
        return {c, d};
    }

    vector<pair<int, int>> convert_to_fan(int pivot) {
        vector<pair<int, int>> flips;
        vector<pair<int, int>> current_diags(diagonals.begin(), diagonals.end());

        auto process = [&](int a, int b) {
            auto [c, d] = find_flip(a, b);
            if (c == -1 || d == -1) return false;
            remove_diagonal(a, b);
            add_diagonal(c, d);
            flips.emplace_back(a, b);
            return true;
        };

        bool changed = true;
        while (changed) {
            changed = false;
            vector<pair<int, int>> to_process;
            for (auto &d : diagonals) {
                if (d.first != pivot && d.second != pivot) {
                    to_process.push_back(d);
                }
            }
            for (auto &d : to_process) {
                if (!diagonals.count(d)) continue;
                if (d.first == pivot || d.second == pivot) continue;
                if (process(d.first, d.second)) {
                    changed = true;
                }
            }
        }
        return flips;
    }
};

vector<pair<int, int>> read_diagonals(int n) {
    vector<pair<int, int>> res(n-3);
    for (int i = 0; i < n-3; ++i) {
        int a, b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        if (a > b) swap(a, b);
        res[i] = {a, b};
    }
    return res;
}

int main() {
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    Triangulation initial(n), target(n);
    auto initial_diags = read_diagonals(n);
    for (auto [a, b] : initial_diags) initial.add_diagonal(a, b);
    auto target_diags = read_diagonals(n);
    for (auto [a, b] : target_diags) target.add_diagonal(a, b);

    auto flips_initial = initial.convert_to_fan(1);
    auto flips_target = target.convert_to_fan(1);

    reverse(flips_target.begin(), flips_target.end());
    for (auto &flip : flips_target) {
        int a = flip.first, b = flip.second;
        Triangulation temp(n);
        temp.add_diagonal(a, b);
        auto [c, d] = temp.find_flip(a, b);
        flip = {c, d};
    }

    cout << flips_initial.size() + flips_target.size() << '\n';
    for (auto [a, b] : flips_initial) cout << a << ' ' << b << '\n';
    for (auto [a, b] : flips_target) cout << a << ' ' << b << '\n';

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n;
    cin >> n;
    vector<int> p(n + 1);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        cin >> p[i];
    }

    // Check if permutation is an involution
    bool is_involution = true;
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        if (p[p[i]] != i) {
            is_involution = false;
            break;
        }
    }
    if (!is_involution) {
        cout << "NO\n";
        return 0;
    }

    // Find cycles
    vector<bool> visited(n + 1, false);
    vector<vector<int>> cycles;
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        if (!visited[i]) {
            vector<int> cycle;
            int current = i;
            while (!visited[current]) {
                visited[current] = true;
                cycle.push_back(current);
                current = p[current];
            }
            if (cycle.size() > 2) {
                cout << "NO\n";
                return 0;
            }
            cycles.push_back(cycle);
        }
    }

    // Separate fixed points and pairs
    vector<int> fixed_points;
    vector<pair<int, int>> pairs;
    for (const auto& cycle : cycles) {
        if (cycle.size() == 1) {
            fixed_points.push_back(cycle[0]);
        } else {
            pairs.emplace_back(cycle[0], cycle[1]);
        }
    }

    if (!fixed_points.empty()) {
        // Build star tree with a fixed point as root
        int root = fixed_points[0];
        vector<pair<int, int>> edges;
        for (const auto& pair : pairs) {
            edges.emplace_back(root, pair.first);
            edges.emplace_back(root, pair.second);
        }
        for (size_t i = 1; i < fixed_points.size(); ++i) {
            edges.emplace_back(root, fixed_points[i]);
        }

        cout << "YES\n";
        for (const auto& edge : edges) {
            cout << edge.first << ' ' << edge.second << '\n';
        }
    } else {
        // All cycles are 2-cycles, build chain
        sort(pairs.begin(), pairs.end());
        vector<pair<int, int>> edges;

        // Connect a_i to a_{i+1}
        for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < pairs.size(); ++i) {
            edges.emplace_back(pairs[i].first, pairs[i + 1].first);
        }

        // Connect last a to last b
        if (!pairs.empty()) {
            edges.emplace_back(pairs.back().first, pairs.back().second);
        }

        // Connect b's in reverse order
        for (size_t i = pairs.size() - 1; i >= 1; --i) {
            edges.emplace_back(pairs[i].second, pairs[i - 1].second);
        }

        if ((int)edges.size() != n - 1) {
            cout << "NO\n";
            return 0;
        }

        cout << "YES\n";
        for (const auto& edge : edges) {
            cout << edge.first << ' ' << edge.second << '\n';
        }
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);
    
    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    
    vector<vector<int>> words(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        int l;
        cin >> l;
        words[i].resize(l);
        for (int j = 0; j < l; ++j) {
            cin >> words[i][j];
        }
    }
    
    vector<bool> must_include(m + 1, false);
    vector<bool> must_exclude(m + 1, false);
    vector<vector<int>> adj(m + 1);
    
    for (int i = 0; i < n - 1; ++i) {
        const auto& a = words[i];
        const auto& b = words[i + 1];
        
        bool is_prefix = true;
        int min_len = min(a.size(), b.size());
        int j;
        for (j = 0; j < min_len; ++j) {
            if (a[j] != b[j]) {
                is_prefix = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (is_prefix) {
            if (a.size() > b.size()) {
                cout << "No\n";
                return 0;
            }
            continue;
        }
        
        int x = a[j];
        int y = b[j];
        if (x > y) {
            must_include[x] = true;
            must_exclude[y] = true;
        } else if (x < y) {
            adj[y].push_back(x);
        }
    }
    
    for (int i = 1; i <= m; ++i) {
        if (must_include[i] && must_exclude[i]) {
            cout << "No\n";
            return 0;
        }
    }
    
    vector<bool> in_S(m + 1, false);
    queue<int> q;
    
    for (int i = 1; i <= m; ++i) {
        if (must_include[i]) {
            if (must_exclude[i]) {
                cout << "No\n";
                return 0;
            }
            if (!in_S[i]) {
                in_S[i] = true;
                q.push(i);
            }
        }
    }
    
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front();
        q.pop();
        
        for (int x : adj[u]) {
            if (must_exclude[x]) {
                cout << "No\n";
                return 0;
            }
            if (!in_S[x]) {
                in_S[x] = true;
                q.push(x);
            }
        }
    }
    
    for (int i = 1; i <= m; ++i) {
        if (must_exclude[i] && in_S[i]) {
            cout << "No\n";
            return 0;
        }
    }
    
    vector<int> result;
    for (int i = 1; i <= m; ++i) {
        if (in_S[i]) {
            result.push_back(i);
        }
    }
    
    cout << "Yes\n";
    cout << result.size() << '\n';
    for (int x : result) {
        cout << x << ' ';
    }
    cout << '\n';
    
    return 0;

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const int INF = 1e9;

struct Edge {
    int to, rev, cap, cost;
};

vector<vector<Edge>> g;
vector<int> pot;

void add_edge(int from, int to, int cap, int cost) {
    g[from].push_back({to, (int)g[to].size(), cap, cost});
    g[to].push_back({from, (int)g[from].size()-1, 0, -cost});
}

pair<int, int> mcf(int s, int t, int maxf) {
    int flow = 0, cost = 0;
    pot.assign(g.size(), 0);
    vector<int> dist, prevedge, prevnode;
    while (flow < maxf) {
        dist.assign(g.size(), INF);
        dist[s] = 0;
        priority_queue<pair<int, int>> pq;
        pq.push({0, s});
        prevedge.assign(g.size(), -1);
        prevnode.assign(g.size(), -1);
        while (!pq.empty()) {
            auto [d, u] = pq.top(); pq.pop();
            d = -d;
            if (d > dist[u]) continue;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < g[u].size(); ++i) {
                auto &e = g[u][i];
                if (e.cap > 0 && dist[e.to] > d + e.cost + pot[u] - pot[e.to]) {
                    dist[e.to] = d + e.cost + pot[u] - pot[e.to];
                    prevnode[e.to] = u;
                    prevedge[e.to] = i;
                    pq.push({-dist[e.to], e.to});
                }
            }
        }
        if (dist[t] == INF) break;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < g.size(); ++i)
            if (dist[i] < INF) pot[i] += dist[i];
        int f = maxf - flow;
        for (int v = t; v != s; v = prevnode[v])
            f = min(f, g[prevnode[v]][prevedge[v]].cap);
        flow += f;
        cost += f * (pot[t] - pot[s]);
        for (int v = t; v != s; v = prevnode[v]) {
            auto &e = g[prevnode[v]][prevedge[v]];
            e.cap -= f;
            g[e.to][e.rev].cap += f;
        }
    }
    return {flow, cost};
}

struct OriginalEdgeInfo {
    int red_node, red_idx, blue_node, blue_idx;
};

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n1, n2, m, r, b;
    cin >> n1 >> n2 >> m >> r >> b;
    string left_colors, right_colors;
    cin >> left_colors >> right_colors;

    vector<int> demand(2 + n1 + n2, 0);
    for (int u = 1; u <= n1; ++u) {
        char c = left_colors[u-1];
        if (c == 'R') demand[1 + u]++;
        else if (c == 'B') demand[1 + u]--;
    }
    for (int v = 1; v <= n2; ++v) {
        char c = right_colors[v-1];
        if (c == 'R') demand[2 + n1 + v - 1]++;
        else if (c == 'B') demand[2 + n1 + v - 1]--;
    }

    int total_demand = 0;
    for (int d : demand) total_demand += d;
    if (total_demand != 0) {
        cout << "-1\n";
        return 0;
    }

    g.resize(2 + n1 + n2);
    vector<OriginalEdgeInfo> edge_info;

    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int u, v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        int L_u = 1 + u, R_v = 2 + n1 + v - 1;
        add_edge(L_u, R_v, 1, r);
        int red_idx = g[L_u].size() - 1;
        add_edge(R_v, L_u, 1, b);
        int blue_idx = g[R_v].size() - 1;
        edge_info.push_back({L_u, red_idx, R_v, blue_idx});
    }

    int total_positive = 0;
    for (int i = 2; i < demand.size(); ++i) {
        if (demand[i] > 0) {
            add_edge(0, i, demand[i], 0);
            total_positive += demand[i];
        } else if (demand[i] < 0) {
            add_edge(i, 1, -demand[i], 0);
        }
    }

    auto [flow, cost] = mcf(0, 1, total_positive);
    if (flow != total_positive) {
        cout << "-1\n";
        return 0;
    }

    string result(m, 'U');
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        auto &info = edge_info[i];
        if (g[info.red_node][info.red_idx].cap == 0)
            result[i] = 'R';
        else if (g[info.blue_node][info.blue_idx].cap == 0)
            result[i] = 'B';
    }

    cout << cost << '\n' << result << '\n';
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const int MAX_PRIME = 2e5;
vector<bool> is_prime;

struct Edge {
    int to, rev;
    long long cap;
    Edge(int to_, int rev_, long long cap_) : to(to_), rev(rev_), cap(cap_) {}
};

vector<vector<Edge>> graph;

void sieve() {
    is_prime.resize(MAX_PRIME + 1, true);
    is_prime[0] = is_prime[1] = false;
    for (int i = 2; i * i <= MAX_PRIME; ++i) {
        if (is_prime[i]) {
            for (int j = i * i; j <= MAX_PRIME; j += i) {
                is_prime[j] = false;
            }
        }
    }
}

struct Card {
    int p, c, l;
};

int main() {
    sieve();
    int n, k;
    cin >> n >> k;
    vector<Card> cards(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        cin >> cards[i].p >> cards[i].c >> cards[i].l;
    }

    int ans = -1;
    int left = 1, right = n;
    while (left <= right) {
        int mid = (left + right) / 2;
        vector<Card> current;
        long long total_p = 0;
        for (auto& card : cards) {
            if (card.l <= mid) {
                current.push_back(card);
                total_p += card.p;
            }
        }
        if (total_p < k) {
            left = mid + 1;
            continue;
        }

        vector<Card> even, odd;
        for (auto& card : current) {
            if (card.c % 2 == 0)
                even.push_back(card);
            else
                odd.push_back(card);
        }

        int m = even.size();
        int o = odd.size();
        int nodes = m + o + 2;
        graph.clear();
        graph.resize(nodes);

        auto add_edge = [&](int from, int to, long long cap) {
            graph[from].emplace_back(to, graph[to].size(), cap);
            graph[to].emplace_back(from, graph[from].size() - 1, 0);
        };

        const long long INF = 1e18;
        for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
            add_edge(0, i + 1, even[i].p);
        }
        for (int j = 0; j < o; ++j) {
            add_edge(m + 1 + j, nodes - 1, odd[j].p);
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < o; ++j) {
                if (is_prime[even[i].c + odd[j].c]) {
                    add_edge(i + 1, m + 1 + j, INF);
                }
            }
        }

        long long max_flow = 0;
        vector<int> level(nodes), iter(nodes);
        while (true) {
            fill(level.begin(), level.end(), -1);
            level[0] = 0;
            queue<int> q;
            q.push(0);
            while (!q.empty()) {
                int u = q.front(); q.pop();
                for (Edge& e : graph[u]) {
                    if (e.cap > 0 && level[e.to] == -1) {
                        level[e.to] = level[u] + 1;
                        q.push(e.to);
                    }
                }
            }
            if (level[nodes - 1] == -1) break;
            fill(iter.begin(), iter.end(), 0);
            function<long long(int, long long)> dfs = [&](int u, long long flow) {
                if (u == nodes - 1) return flow;
                for (int& i = iter[u]; i < graph[u].size(); ++i) {
                    Edge& e = graph[u][i];
                    if (e.cap > 0 && level[e.to] == level[u] + 1) {
                        long long f = dfs(e.to, min(flow, e.cap));
                        if (f > 0) {
                            e.cap -= f;
                            graph[e.to][e.rev].cap += f;
                            return f;
                        }
                    }
                }
                return 0LL;
            };
            long long f;
            while ((f = dfs(0, INF)) > 0) {
                max_flow += f;
            }
        }

        if (total_p - max_flow >= k) {
            ans = mid;
            right = mid - 1;
        } else {
            left = mid + 1;
        }
    }

    cout << ans << endl;

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

vector<vector<int>> adj;
vector<int> leaves;
vector<bool> removed;
vector<int> parent;

pair<int, int> bfs(int start, int n) {
    vector<int> dist(n+1, -1);
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(start);
    dist[start] = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front();
        q.pop();
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (!removed[v] && dist[v] == -1 && v != parent[u]) {
                dist[v] = dist[u] + 1;
                parent[v] = u;
                q.push(v);
            }
        }
    }
    int max_dist = -1, node = start;
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        if (!removed[i] && dist[i] > max_dist) {
            max_dist = dist[i];
            node = i;
        }
    }
    return {node, max_dist};
}

pair<int, int> get_diam(int n) {
    parent.assign(n+1, -1);
    int u = bfs(1, n).first;
    parent.assign(n+1, -1);
    auto [v, d] = bfs(u, n);
    return {u, v};
}

bool is_leaf(int u) {
    if (removed[u]) return false;
    int cnt = 0;
    for (int v : adj[u]) {
        if (!removed[v] && v != parent[u]) ++cnt;
    }
    return cnt == 0;
}

void find_leaves(int n, vector<int>& curr_leaves) {
    curr_leaves.clear();
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        if (!removed[i] && is_leaf(i)) {
            curr_leaves.push_back(i);
        }
    }
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);
    int n;
    cin >> n;
    adj.resize(n+1);
    for (int i = 0; i < n-1; ++i) {
        int u, v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        adj[u].push_back(v);
        adj[v].push_back(u);
    }
    removed.assign(n+1, false);
    vector<tuple<int, int, int>> operations;
    int total = 0;
    vector<int> curr_leaves;

    for (int step = 0; step < n-1; ++step) {
        auto [u, v] = get_diam(n);
        find_leaves(n, curr_leaves);
        int a = -1, b = -1;
        int max_dist = -1;
        for (int x : curr_leaves) {
            for (int y : curr_leaves) {
                if (x >= y) continue;
                parent.assign(n+1, -1);
                bfs(x, n);
                vector<int> path;
                int tmp = y;
                while (tmp != -1) {
                    path.push_back(tmp);
                    tmp = parent[tmp];
                }
                int dist = path.size() - 1;
                if (dist > max_dist) {
                    max_dist = dist;
                    a = x;
                    b = y;
                }
            }
        }
        if (a == -1) {
            a = curr_leaves[0];
            b = curr_leaves[1];
        }
        total += max_dist;
        int remove_node = b;
        removed[remove_node] = true;
        operations.emplace_back(a, b, remove_node);
    }

    cout << total << '\n';
    for (auto [a, b, c] : operations) {
        cout << a << ' ' << b << ' ' << c << '\n';
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <iostream>
#include <string>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

string multiply(string num, int x) {
    reverse(num.begin(), num.end());
    string res;
    int carry = 0;
    for (char c : num) {
        int digit = c - '0';
        int product = digit * x + carry;
        res.push_back(product % 10 + '0');
        carry = product / 10;
    }
    while (carry > 0) {
        res.push_back(carry % 10 + '0');
        carry /= 10;
    }
    reverse(res.begin(), res.end());
    return res;
}

string divide(string num, int x) {
    string res;
    int carry = 0;
    bool leading_zero = true;
    for (char c : num) {
        int digit = c - '0';
        int current = carry * 10 + digit;
        int q = current / x;
        carry = current % x;
        if (leading_zero && q == 0) {
            continue;
        }
        leading_zero = false;
        res.push_back(q + '0');
    }
    if (res.empty()) {
        res = "0";
    }
    return res;
}

int compare(const string& a, const string& b) {
    if (a.size() != b.size()) {
        return a.size() > b.size() ? 1 : -1;
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < a.size(); ++i) {
        if (a[i] != b[i]) {
            return a[i] > b[i] ? 1 : -1;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

string comb(int a, int b) {
    if (b == 0 || b == a) {
        return "1";
    }
    if (b > a) {
        return "0";
    }
    string result = "1";
    for (int i = 1; i <= b; ++i) {
        int numerator = a - b + i;
        result = multiply(result, numerator);
        result = divide(result, i);
    }
    return result;
}

string subtract(string a, string b) {
    reverse(a.begin(), a.end());
    reverse(b.begin(), b.end());
    string res;
    int carry = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < a.size(); ++i) {
        int digit_a = a[i] - '0';
        int digit_b = (i < b.size() ? b[i] - '0' : 0);
        int sub = digit_a - digit_b - carry;
        if (sub < 0) {
            sub += 10;
            carry = 1;
        } else {
            carry = 0;
        }
        res.push_back(sub + '0');
    }
    while (res.size() > 1 && res.back() == '0') {
        res.pop_back();
    }
    reverse(res.begin(), res.end());
    int pos = 0;
    while (pos < res.size() && res[pos] == '0') {
        pos++;
    }
    if (pos == res.size()) {
        return "0";
    }
    return res.substr(pos);
}

string build_string(int n, int k, string m) {
    string res;
    int remaining_ones = k;
    int remaining_length = n;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        if (remaining_ones == 0) {
            res.append(remaining_length, '0');
            break;
        }
        if (remaining_ones == remaining_length) {
            res.append(remaining_length, '1');
            break;
        }
        int a = remaining_length - 1;
        int b = remaining_ones;
        string c = comb(a, b);
        if (compare(c, m) >= 0) {
            res += '0';
        } else {
            res += '1';
            m = subtract(m, c);
            remaining_ones--;
        }
        remaining_length--;
    }
    return res;
}

int main() {
    int T;
    cin >> T;
    while (T--) {
        int N, K;
        string M;
        cin >> N >> K >> M;

        int pos = 0;
        while (pos < M.size() && M[pos] == '0') {
            pos++;
        }
        if (pos == M.size()) {
            M = "0";
        } else {
            M = M.substr(pos);
        }

        string total = comb(N, K);
        if (compare(total, M) < 0) {
            cout << -1 << endl;
        } else {
            string s = build_string(N, K, M);
            cout << s << endl;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Point {
    int x, y;
    Point() {}
    Point(int x, int y) : x(x), y(y) {}
    bool operator==(const Point& p) const { return x == p.x && y == p.y; }
};

vector<Point> points;
vector<Point> hull;
int N, K;
vector<vector<Point>> validHulls;

// Cross product to determine the orientation of the triplet (o, a, b)
int cross(const Point& o, const Point& a, const Point& b) {
    return (a.x - o.x) * (b.y - o.y) - (a.y - o.y) * (b.x - o.x);
}

// Polar angle comparison
bool compare(const Point& a, const Point& b) {
    int c = cross(points[0], a, b);
    if (c == 0) {
        return (a.x - points[0].x)*(a.x - points[0].x) + (a.y - points[0].y)*(a.y - points[0].y) < 
               (b.x - points[0].x)*(b.x - points[0].x) + (b.y - points[0].y)*(b.y - points[0].y);
    }
    return c > 0;
}

// Check if point p is on the segment (a, b)
bool onSegment(Point a, Point p, Point b) {
    return min(a.x, b.x) <= p.x && p.x <= max(a.x, b.x) &&
           min(a.y, b.y) <= p.y && p.y <= max(a.y, b.y) &&
           cross(a, p, b) == 0;
}

// Check if point p is inside the convex polygon (using ray casting)
bool isInside(const vector<Point>& poly, Point p) {
    int n = poly.size();
    if (n == 0) return false;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        if (p == poly[i] || onSegment(poly[i], p, poly[(i+1)%n])) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    bool inside = false;
    for (int i = 0, j = n-1; i < n; j = i++) {
        if (((poly[i].y > p.y) != (poly[j].y > p.y)) &&
            (p.x < (poly[j].x - poly[i].x) * (p.y - poly[i].y) / (double)(poly[j].y - poly[i].y) + poly[i].x))
            inside = !inside;
    }
    return inside;
}

// Generate all convex hulls of size K and check if they are empty
void generateCombinations(int start, vector<Point>& current) {
    if (current.size() == K) {
        vector<Point> temp = current;
        vector<Point> convHull;
        int m = temp.size();
        if (m <= 2) return;
        sort(temp.begin(), temp.end(), [](const Point& a, const Point& b) {
            return a.x < b.x || (a.x == b.x && a.y < b.y);
        });
        vector<Point> up, down;
        for (auto& p : temp) {
            while (up.size() >= 2 && cross(up[up.size()-2], up.back(), p) >= 0)
                up.pop_back();
            up.push_back(p);
            while (down.size() >= 2 && cross(down[down.size()-2], down.back(), p) <= 0)
                down.pop_back();
            down.push_back(p);
        }
        convHull = up;
        for (int i = down.size()-2; i > 0; --i)
            convHull.push_back(down[i]);
        if (convHull.size() != K) return;
        bool valid = true;
        for (auto& p : points) {
            if (find(temp.begin(), temp.end(), p) != temp.end()) continue;
            if (isInside(convHull, p)) {
                valid = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (valid) {
            validHulls.push_back(convHull);
        }
        return;
    }
    for (int i = start; i < points.size(); ++i) {
        current.push_back(points[i]);
        generateCombinations(i+1, current);
        current.pop_back();
    }
}

double computeArea(const vector<Point>& poly) {
    double area = 0.0;
    int n = poly.size();
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        int j = (i + 1) % n;
        area += (poly[i].x * poly[j].y - poly[j].x * poly[i].y);
    }
    return fabs(area) / 2.0;
}

int main() {
    cin >> N >> K;
    points.resize(N);
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        cin >> points[i].x >> points[i].y;
    }
    if (K < 3) {
        cout << "0.00" << endl;
        return 0;
    }
    vector<Point> current;
    generateCombinations(0, current);
    double maxArea = 0.0;
    for (auto& hull : validHulls) {
        double area = computeArea(hull);
        if (area > maxArea) {
            maxArea = area;
        }
    }
    cout << fixed << setprecision(2) << maxArea << endl;
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>

int n;

int query(int x1, int y1, int x2, int y2) {
    cout << "? " << x1 << ' ' << y1 << ' ' << x2 << ' ' << y2 << endl;
    cout.flush();
    int res;
    cin >> res;
    return res;
}

int find_min_x() {
    int left = 1, right = n, ans = n + 1;
    while (left <= right) {
        int mid = (left + right) / 2;
        int res = query(mid, 1, n, n);
        if (res == 2) {
            ans = mid;
            right = mid - 1;
        } else {
            left = mid + 1;
        }
    }
    return ans;
}

int find_max_x() {
    int left = 1, right = n, ans = 0;
    while (left <= right) {
        int mid = (left + right) / 2;
        int res = query(1, 1, mid, n);
        if (res == 2) {
            ans = mid;
            left = mid + 1;
        } else {
            right = mid - 1;
        }
    }
    return ans;
}

int find_min_y() {
    int left = 1, right = n, ans = n + 1;
    while (left <= right) {
        int mid = (left + right) / 2;
        int res = query(1, mid, n, n);
        if (res == 2) {
            ans = mid;
            right = mid - 1;
        } else {
            left = mid + 1;
        }
    }
    return ans;
}

int find_max_y() {
    int left = 1, right = n, ans = 0;
    while (left <= right) {
        int mid = (left + right) / 2;
        int res = query(1, 1, n, mid);
        if (res == 2) {
            ans = mid;
            left = mid + 1;
        } else {
            right = mid - 1;
        }
    }
    return ans;
}

struct Rect {
    int x1, y1, x2, y2;
    Rect(int a, int b, int c, int d) : x1(a), y1(b), x2(c), y2(d) {}
};

Rect find_rect(int x_a, int x_b, int y_a, int y_b) {
    int y1 = y_a;
    int left = y_a, right = y_b;
    while (left <= right) {
        int mid = (left + right) / 2;
        int res = query(x_a, mid, x_b, y_b);
        if (res >= 1) {
            y1 = mid;
            right = mid - 1;
        } else {
            left = mid + 1;
        }
    }
    int y2 = y1;
    left = y1, right = y_b;
    while (left <= right) {
        int mid = (left + right) / 2;
        int res = query(x_a, y1, x_b, mid);
        if (res >= 1) {
            y2 = mid;
            left = mid + 1;
        } else {
            right = mid - 1;
        }
    }
    return Rect(x_a, y1, x_b, y2);
}

        int res2 = query(split_x + 1, y_low, x_high, y_high);
        if (res2 == 1) {
            split_x_valid = true;
        }
    }
    if (split_x_valid) {
        Rect rect1 = find_rect(x_low, split_x, y_low, y_high);
        Rect rect2 = find_rect(split_x + 1, x_high, y_low, y_high);
        cout << "! " << rect1.x1 << ' ' << rect1.y1 << ' ' << rect1.x2 << ' ' << rect1.y2 << ' '
             << rect2.x1 << ' ' << rect2.y1 << ' ' << rect2.x2 << ' ' << rect2.y2 << endl;
        cout.flush();

    l = y_low, r = y_high;
    while (l <= r) {
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        int res = query(x_low, y_low, x_high, mid);
        if (res == 1) {
            split_y = mid;
            r = mid - 1;
        } else if (res == 2) {
            l = mid + 1;
        } else {
            r = mid - 1;

        if (res2 == 1) {
            split_y_valid = true;

        Rect rect2 = find_rect(x_low, x_high, split_y + 1, y_high);

             << rect2.x1 << ' ' << rect2.y1 << ' ' << rect2.x2 << ' ' << rect2.y2 << endl;
        cout.flush();
        return 0;
    }

    return 0;

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

typedef long long ll;

struct TestCase {
    int n;
    vector<pair<ll, ll>> x;
    vector<pair<ll, ll>> y;
    vector<pair<ll, ll>> z;
};

vector<TestCase> read_input() {
    int t;
    cin >> t;
    vector<TestCase> cases(t);
    for (int i = 0; i < t; ++i) {
        TestCase& tc = cases[i];
        cin >> tc.n;
        tc.x.resize(tc.n);
        for (int j = 0; j < tc.n; ++j)
            cin >> tc.x[j].first >> tc.x[j].second;
        tc.y.resize(tc.n - 1);
        for (int j = 0; j < tc.n - 1; ++j)
            cin >> tc.y[j].first >> tc.y[j].second;
        tc.z.resize(tc.n - 2);
        for (int j = 0; j < tc.n - 2; ++j)
            cin >> tc.z[j].first >> tc.z[j].second;
    }
    return cases;
}

void solve() {
    vector<TestCase> cases = read_input();
    for (const auto& tc : cases) {
        int n = tc.n;
        const auto& x = tc.x;
        const auto& y = tc.y;
        const auto& z = tc.z;

        if (n < 3) {
            cout << "NO\n";
            continue;
        }

        ll delta_low = y[0].first;
        ll delta_high = y[0].second;
        if (delta_low > delta_high) {
            cout << "NO\n";
            continue;
        }

        vector<double> s_min(n + 1), s_max(n + 1);
        s_min[1] = 0.0;
        s_max[1] = 0.0;
        s_min[2] = delta_low;
        s_max[2] = delta_high;

        bool possible = true;
        for (int i = 3; i <= n; ++i) {
            int y_idx = i - 2;
            int z_idx = i - 3;
            if (y_idx >= (int)y.size() || z_idx >= (int)z.size()) {
                possible = false;
                break;
            }

            ll y_lo = y[y_idx].first;
            ll y_hi = y[y_idx].second;
            ll z_lo = z[z_idx].first;
            ll z_hi = z[z_idx].second;

            ll new_lo = max(y_lo, delta_low + z_lo);
            ll new_hi = min(y_hi, delta_high + z_hi);
            if (new_lo > new_hi) {
                possible = false;
                break;
            }

            delta_low = new_lo;
            delta_high = new_hi;
            s_min[i] = s_min[i - 1] + delta_low;
            s_max[i] = s_max[i - 1] + delta_high;
        }

        if (!possible) {
            cout << "NO\n";
            continue;
        }

        double global_lo = -1e18;
        double global_hi = 1e18;
        for (int k = 1; k <= n; ++k) {
            double curr_lo, curr_hi;
            if (k == 1) {
                curr_lo = x[k - 1].first;
                curr_hi = x[k - 1].second;
            } else {
                curr_lo = x[k - 1].first - s_max[k];
                curr_hi = x[k - 1].second - s_min[k];
            }
            global_lo = max(global_lo, curr_lo);
            global_hi = min(global_hi, curr_hi);
            if (global_lo > global_hi) {
                possible = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        cout << (possible && global_lo <= global_hi ? "YES\n" : "NO\n");
    }
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);
    solve();
    return 0;
}<#sep#>You are tasked with implementing a `BankAccount` class in Python that simulates basic banking operations. The class should support the following functionalities:

- **Deposit**: Add a specified amount to the account balance. The amount must be positive.
- **Withdraw**: Subtract a specified amount from the account balance, ensuring the balance does not go negative. The amount must be positive.
- **Get Balance**: Return the current balance of the account.
- **Get Transactions**: Return a list of all transactions, where each transaction is a string indicating the amount and the type (deposit or withdrawal).
- **Get Statement**: Return a formatted string that includes the account holder's name, account number, current balance, and a list of all transactions.

Your implementation should handle invalid inputs gracefully by raising appropriate exceptions.

**Sample Input/Output**:
```python
account = BankAccount("John Doe", "123456789")
account.deposit(100)
account.withdraw(50)
print(account.get_balance())  # Output: 50
print(account.get_transactions())  # Output: ['Deposited: 100', 'Withdrew: 50']
print(account.get_statement())
# Expected Output:
# Account Holder: John Doe
# Account Number: 123456789
# Current Balance: 50
# Transactions:
#   Deposited: 100
#   Withdrew: 50
```

```python
class BankAccount:
    def __init__(self, account_holder_name, account_number):
        """
        Initialize a new bank account with the given account holder's name and account number.
        
        :param account_holder_name: The name of the account holder.
        :param account_number: The account number.
        """
        self.account_holder_name = account_holder_name
        self.account_number = account_number
        self.balance = 0
        self.transactions = []

    def deposit(self, amount):
        """
        Deposit a specified amount into the account.
        
        :param amount: The amount to deposit.
        :raises ValueError: If the amount is not positive.
        """
        if amount > 0:
            self.balance += amount
            self.transactions.append(f"Deposited: {amount}")
        else:
            raise ValueError("Deposit amount must be positive.")

    def withdraw(self, amount):
        """
        Withdraw a specified amount from the account.
        
        :param amount: The amount to withdraw.
        :raises ValueError: If the amount is not positive.
        :raises ValueError: If the amount exceeds the current balance.
        """
        if amount > self.balance:
            print("Insufficient funds. Withdrawal not allowed.")
        elif amount <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Withdrawal amount must be positive.")
        else:
            self.balance -= amount
            self.transactions.append(f"Withdrew: {amount}")

    def get_balance(self):
        """
        Get the current balance of the account.
        
        :return: The current balance.
        """
        return self.balance

    def get_transactions(self):
        """
        Get a list of all transactions.
        
        :return: A list of transaction strings.
        """
        return self.transactions

    def get_statement(self):
        """
        Get a formatted statement of the account.
        
        :return: A formatted string with account details and transactions.
        """
        statement = (f"Account Holder: {self.account_holder_name}\n"
                     f"Account Number: {self.account_number}\n"
                     f"Current Balance: {self.balance}\n"
                     "Transactions:\n")
        for transaction in self.transactions:
            statement += f"  {transaction}\n"
        return statement
```<#sep#>You are tasked with developing a RESTful API using Flask for a web application that supports basic CRUD operations on a collection of items. Your API must include the following endpoints:

- `GET /items`: Retrieve a list of all items.
- `GET /items/<id>`: Retrieve a specific item by its ID. If the item does not exist, return a 404 error.
- `POST /items`: Create a new item. The request body should be a JSON object containing the item data. If the request body is invalid, return a 400 error.
- `PUT /items/<id>`: Update an existing item. The request body should be a JSON object containing the updated item data. If the item does not exist or the request body is invalid, return a 404 or 400 error, respectively.
- `DELETE /items/<id>`: Delete an item by its ID. If the item does not exist, return a 404 error.

**Sample Input/Output:**

1. **GET /items**
   - Response: `{"1": {"name": "Item 1", "description": "This is item 1"}, "2": {"name": "Item 2", "description": "This is item 2"}}`

2. **GET /items/1**
   - Response: `{"name": "Item 1", "description": "This is item 1"}`

3. **POST /items**
   - Request Body: `{"name": "Item 3", "description": "This is item 3"}`
   - Response: `{"name": "Item 3", "description": "This is item 3"}`

4. **PUT /items/1**
   - Request Body: `{"name": "Updated Item 1", "description": "Updated description"}`
   - Response: `{"name": "Updated Item 1", "description": "Updated description"}`

5. **DELETE /items/2**
   - Response: `{"result": true}`

```python
from flask import Flask, jsonify, request, abort

app = Flask(__name__)

# Sample data as a dictionary to mimic a database
items = {
    1: {'name': 'Item 1', 'description': 'This is item 1'},
    2: {'name': 'Item 2', 'description': 'This is item 2'}
}

def find_item(item_id):
    """
    Helper function to find an item by its ID.
    
    :param item_id: ID of the item to find.
    :return: Item dictionary if found, otherwise None.
    """
    item_id = int(item_id)
    return items.get(item_id)

@app.route('/items', methods=['GET'])
def get_items():
    """
    Retrieve a list of all items.
    
    :return: JSON response containing all items.
    """
    return jsonify(items)

@app.route('/items/<id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_item(id):
    """
    Retrieve a specific item by its ID.
    
    :param id: ID of the item to retrieve.
    :return: JSON response containing the item if found, otherwise 404 error.
    """
    item = find_item(id)
    if item is None:
        abort(404)
    return jsonify(item)

@app.route('/items', methods=['POST'])
def create_item():
    """
    Create a new item.
    
    :return: JSON response containing the created item and 201 status code if successful, otherwise 400 error.
    """
    if not request.json or 'name' not in request.json:
        abort(400)
    item_id = max(items.keys()) + 1 if items else 1
    item = {
        'name': request.json['name'],
        'description': request.json.get('description', "")
    }
    items[item_id] = item
    return jsonify(item), 201

@app.route('/items/<id>', methods=['PUT'])
def update_item(id):
    """
    Update an existing item.
    
    :param id: ID of the item to update.
    :return: JSON response containing the updated item if successful, otherwise 404 or 400 error.
    """
    item = find_item(id)
    if item is None:
        abort(404)
    if not request.json:
        abort(400)
    item['name'] = request.json.get('name', item['name'])
    item['description'] = request.json.get('description', item['description'])
    return jsonify(item)

@app.route('/items/<id>', methods=['DELETE'])
def delete_item(id):
    """
    Delete an item by its ID.
    
    :param id: ID of the item to delete.
    :return: JSON response indicating successful deletion if item exists, otherwise 404 error.
    """
    item = find_item(id)
    if item is None:
        abort(404)
    del items[int(id)]
    return jsonify({'result': True})

@app.errorhandler(404)
def not_found(error):
    """
    Handle 404 Not Found errors.
    
    :param error: Error object.
    :return: JSON response with error message and 404 status code.
    """
    return jsonify({'error': 'Not found'}), 404

@app.errorhandler(400)
def bad_request(error):
    """
    Handle 400 Bad Request errors.
    
    :param error: Error object.
    :return: JSON response with error message and 400 status code.
    """
    return jsonify({'error': 'Bad request'}), 400

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app.run(debug=True)
```<#sep#>You are given a dictionary `data` where each key is a book title and the value is another dictionary containing details about the book such as `author`, `year`, and `pages`. Implement a function `get_books_by_author(data, author)` that returns a list of book titles written by the specified author. 

**Input:**
- A dictionary `data` with book titles as keys and dictionaries as values. Each value dictionary contains keys `author`, `year`, and `pages`.
- A string `author` representing the name of the author.

**Output:**
- A list of book titles written by the specified author.

**Sample Input:**
```python
data = {
    "The Great Gatsby": {"author": "F. Scott Fitzgerald", "year": 1925, "pages": 180},
    "To Kill a Mockingbird": {"author": "Harper Lee", "year": 1960, "pages": 281},
    "1984": {"author": "George Orwell", "year": 1949, "pages": 328},
    "Pride and Prejudice": {"author": "Jane Austen", "year": 1813, "pages": 432},
    "The Catcher in the Rye": {"author": "J.D. Salinger", "year": 1951, "pages": 277},
    "Brave New World": {"author": "Aldous Huxley", "year": 1932, "pages": 311},
    "Animal Farm": {"author": "George Orwell", "year": 1945, "pages": 112},
}
author = "George Orwell"
```

**Sample Output:**
```python
['1984', 'Animal Farm']
```

```python
def get_books_by_author(data, author):
    """
    Returns a list of book titles written by the specified author.

    :param data: Dictionary with book titles as keys and dictionaries as values.
                 Each value dictionary contains keys 'author', 'year', and 'pages'.
    :param author: String representing the name of the author.
    :return: List of book titles written by the specified author.
    """
    return [title for title, book_info in data.items() if book_info['author'] == author]

# Example usage:
data = {
    "The Great Gatsby": {"author": "F. Scott Fitzgerald", "year": 1925, "pages": 180},
    "To Kill a Mockingbird": {"author": "Harper Lee", "year": 1960, "pages": 281},
    "1984": {"author": "George Orwell", "year": 1949, "pages": 328},
    "Pride and Prejudice": {"author": "Jane Austen", "year": 1813, "pages": 432},
    "The Catcher in the Rye": {"author": "J.D. Salinger", "year": 1951, "pages": 277},
    "Brave New World": {"author": "Aldous Huxley", "year": 1932, "pages": 311},
    "Animal Farm": {"author": "George Orwell", "year": 1945, "pages": 112},
}

# Testing the function
print(get_books_by_author(data, "George Orwell"))  # Output: ['1984', 'Animal Farm']
print(get_books_by_author(data, "Harper Lee"))     # Output: ['To Kill a Mockingbird']
```<#sep#>You are tasked with designing a RESTful API for a library system using Flask. The system should support the following operations:
- Adding a new book to the library.
- Retrieving all books from the library.
- Retrieving a specific book by its ID.
- Updating the details of a specific book.
- Deleting a specific book from the library.

The books are stored in a SQLite database. The API should handle JSON requests and responses. Implement the API endpoints as specified below.

**Endpoints:**
1. `POST /books` - Add a new book. The request body should contain a JSON object with the following fields: `title`, `author`, and `published_year`.
2. `GET /books` - Retrieve all books. The response should be a JSON array of book objects, each containing the fields: `id`, `title`, `author`, and `published_year`.
3. `GET /books/<book_id>` - Retrieve a specific book by its ID. The response should be a JSON object with the fields: `id`, `title`, `author`, and `published_year`.
4. `PUT /books/<book_id>` - Update the details of a specific book. The request body should contain a JSON object with the fields to be updated: `title`, `author`, and/or `published_year`.
5. `DELETE /books/<book_id>` - Delete a specific book by its ID.

**Sample Input/Output:**
1. `POST /books` with body `{"title": "1984", "author": "George Orwell", "published_year": 1949}` should return `{"message": "Book added successfully"}` with status code 201.
2. `GET /books` should return a JSON array of book objects.
3. `GET /books/1` should return a JSON object representing the book with ID 1.
4. `PUT /books/1` with body `{"title": "Animal Farm"}` should return `{"message": "Book updated successfully"}`.
5. `DELETE /books/1` should return `{"message": "Book deleted successfully"}`.

```python
from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
from flask_sqlalchemy import SQLAlchemy

app = Flask(__name__)
app.config['SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI'] = 'sqlite:///books.db'
app.config['SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS'] = False
db = SQLAlchemy(app)

class Book(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    title = db.Column(db.String(80), nullable=False)
    author = db.Column(db.String(80), nullable=False)
    published_year = db.Column(db.Integer, nullable=True)

    def __repr__(self):
        return f'<Book {self.title}>'

with app.app_context():
    db.create_all()

@app.route('/books', methods=['POST'])
def add_book():
    """
    Adds a new book to the library.
    Expects a JSON object with 'title', 'author', and 'published_year'.
    Returns a success message with status code 201.
    """
    data = request.get_json()
    new_book = Book(title=data.get('title'), author=data.get('author'), published_year=data.get('published_year'))
    db.session.add(new_book)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'message': 'Book added successfully'}), 201

@app.route('/books', methods=['GET'])
def get_books():
    """
    Retrieves all books from the library.
    Returns a JSON array of book objects.
    """
    books = Book.query.all()
    return jsonify([{'id': book.id, 'title': book.title, 'author': book.author, 'published_year': book.published_year} for book in books])

@app.route('/books/<int:book_id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_book(book_id):
    """
    Retrieves a specific book by its ID.
    Returns a JSON object representing the book.
    """
    book = Book.query.get_or_404(book_id)
    return jsonify({'id': book.id, 'title': book.title, 'author': book.author, 'published_year': book.published_year})

@app.route('/books/<int:book_id>', methods=['PUT'])
def update_book(book_id):
    """
    Updates the details of a specific book.
    Expects a JSON object with fields to be updated.
    Returns a success message.
    """
    data = request.get_json()
    book = Book.query.get_or_404(book_id)
    book.title = data.get('title', book.title)
    book.author = data.get('author', book.author)
    book.published_year = data.get('published_year', book.published_year)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'message': 'Book updated successfully'})

@app.route('/books/<int:book_id>', methods=['DELETE'])
def delete_book(book_id):
    """
    Deletes a specific book by its ID.
    Returns a success message.
    """
    book = Book.query.get_or_404(book_id)
    db.session.delete(book)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'message': 'Book deleted successfully'})

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app.run(debug=True)

```python
def generate_html_report(data):
    """
    Generates an HTML report from a dictionary of book information.

    Parameters:
    data (dict): A dictionary where each key is a book title and each value is another dictionary
                 containing 'author', 'year', and 'pages'.

    Returns:
    None: Writes the HTML content to a file named 'book_report.html'.
    """
    html_content = """
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <title>Book Information Report</title>
    <style>
        table {
            width: 50%;
            border-collapse: collapse;
            margin: 25px 0;
            font-size: 18px;
            text-align: left;
        }
        th, td {
            padding: 12px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid #ddd;
        }
        th {
            background-color: #f2f2f2;
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <h1>Book Information Report</h1>
    <table>
        <tr>
            <th>Title</th>
            <th>Author</th>
            <th>Year</th>
            <th>Pages</th>
        </tr>
    """
    
    for title, details in data.items():
        html_content += f"""
        <tr>
            <td>{title}</td>
            <td>{details['author']}</td>
            <td>{details['year']}</td>
            <td>{details['pages']}</td>
        </tr>
        """
    
    html_content += """
    </table>
</body>
</html>
    """
    
    with open("book_report.html", "w") as file:
        file.write(html_content)
```<#sep#>You are tasked with implementing a `BankAccount` class that simulates basic bank account operations. The class should support the following functionalities:

- **Initialization**: Create a bank account with a given account holder's name and an initial balance.
- **Deposit**: Add a specified amount to the account balance. Ensure the deposit amount is positive.
- **Withdraw**: Subtract a specified amount from the account balance if sufficient funds are available. Ensure the withdrawal amount is positive.
- **Check Balance**: Return the current balance of the account.
- **Display Holder Name**: Print the name of the account holder.

**Input/Output Specifications**:
- The `__init__` method will be called with a string `name` and a float `balance`.
- The `deposit` method will be called with a float `amount`.
- The `withdraw` method will be called with a float `amount`.
- The `check_balance` method will return a float.
- The `display_holder_name` method will print the account holder's name.

**Sample Input/Output**:
```python
account = BankAccount("John Doe", 100.0)
account.deposit(50.0)          # No output, balance is now 150.0
account.withdraw(20.0)         # No output, balance is now 130.0
account.withdraw(200.0)        # Output: Insufficient funds.
account.check_balance()        # Returns: 130.0
account.display_holder_name()  # Output: John Doe
```

```python
class BankAccount:
    def __init__(self, name, balance):
        """
        Initialize a new bank account with the given name and balance.

        :param name: str, the name of the account holder
        :param balance: float, the initial balance of the account
        """
        self.name = name
        self.balance = balance

    def deposit(self, amount):
        """
        Deposit a specified amount into the account.

        :param amount: float, the amount to deposit
        """
        if amount > 0:
            self.balance += amount
        else:
            print("Deposit amount should be positive.")

    def withdraw(self, amount):
        """
        Withdraw a specified amount from the account if sufficient funds are available.

        :param amount: float, the amount to withdraw
        """
        if amount > self.balance:
            print("Insufficient funds.")
        elif amount <= 0:
            print("Withdrawal amount should be positive.")
        else:
            self.balance -= amount

    def check_balance(self):
        """
        Return the current balance of the account.

        :return: float, the current balance
        """
        return self.balance

    def display_holder_name(self):
        """
        Print the name of the account holder.
        """
        print(self.name)
```<#sep#>You are tasked with analyzing user behavior on a website using a dataset that includes user interactions with timestamps and event types. Implement a function `analyze_user_behavior(data)` that processes this dataset and returns the following statistics:

1. The number of unique users.
2. The average time spent on the website per user in minutes.

4. A list of the top 5 most visited pages.

**Input:**
- A list of dictionaries, where each dictionary represents an interaction with the following keys:
  - `user_id`: An integer representing the user.
  - `timestamp`: A string representing the time of the interaction in the format 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS'.
  - `event_type`: A string representing the type of event (e.g., 'login', 'page_view', 'logout').
  - `page`: A string representing the page visited, or `None` if the event is not a page view.

**Output:**
- A tuple containing:
  1. An integer representing the number of unique users.
  2. A float representing the average time spent on the website per user in minutes.
  3. A string representing the most common event type.
  4. A list of strings representing the top 5 most visited pages.

**Sample Input:**
```python
data = [
    {'user_id': 1, 'timestamp': '2023-10-01 12:00:00', 'event_type': 'login', 'page': None},
    {'user_id': 1, 'timestamp': '2023-10-01 12:05:00', 'event_type': 'page_view', 'page': 'home.html'},
